### Video resources
![](https://help.webex.com/images/view-thumbnail.svg)Show more
![](https://help.webex.com/images/view-thumbnail.svg)Show more
![Scroll left to see more video resources](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)![Scroll right to see more video resources](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Cisco AI Assistant in meetings
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Sometimes you may join a meeting late, need to step away for a moment, or you're multitasking and get distracted. With AI Assistant meeting summaries, you can quickly catch up on what you’ve missed, understand the discussion, ask questions without disturbing the meeting, and stay on top of the conversation. You also receive a summary and transcript after the meeting, without having to record.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Create an autonomous AI agent
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Customer Experience
Autonomous AI agents operate independently without direct human intervention. Autonomous agents can access and use a knowledge repository to provide informative and accurate answers to user queries. These agents use advanced algorithms and machine learning techniques to analyze data, learn from their environment, and adapt their actions to achieve specific goals.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Share content in a meeting
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
When you share content in Webex App, everyone in the meeting can easily see what's being discussed. Anyone can share content, but only one person can share at a time.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Enhance efficiency using AI-generated summaries
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Customer Experience
The Cisco AI Assistant offers concise summaries of prior interactions, allowing agents to continue conversations seamlessly without needing to re-collect information. This immediate access enables agents to focus on delivering effective solutions, leading to faster resolutions and a more cohesive customer experience.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
Webex App | Add your profile picture
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Make it easier for people to recognize you in Webex App by adding a picture to your profile. Until you upload one, your profile only shows your initials inside a circle.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
AI Assistant wellness break notifications
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Customer Experience
When the AI Assistant recommends a wellbeing break, a toaster notification will appear: Accepting a break: Click Take a break to accept the break. The Cisco AI Assistant widget opens automatically to confirm your choice. After you wrap up the current interaction, your 1-minute wellbeing break begins automatically. Your status changes to Wellbeing Break to prevent the next interaction from being queued to you. A 5-second countdown appears on the screen as your 1-minute break concludes. Once complete, the break window closes automatically, and your status changes to Available, readying you for the next interaction.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)
Generate space summaries
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
When you're busy, or you've been away from the office, catching up with all your spaces can be challenging. AI Assistant can generate space summaries to help you quickly catch up on missed messages and conversations in the space. Stay informed on decisions, key points, and get up to date with the discussion at a glance.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle8](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Enhance efficiency with real-time transcripts
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Customer Experience
Real-time transcripts is designed to help you optimize communication efficiency during customer interactions. By providing live transcriptions directly on your Agent Desktop, it can help enhance your focus, clarity, and response accuracy. It is aimed at enhancing your understanding and efficiency during calls, reducing misunderstandings, and supporting you in delivering high-quality interactions that improve performance and customer satisfaction.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle9](https://help.webex.com/images/video-nine-home.png)
Use the whiteboard in meetings
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
When you're busy, or you've been away from the office, catching up with all your spaces can be challenging. AI Assistant can generate space summaries to help you quickly catch up on missed messages and conversations in the space. Stay informed on decisions, key points, and get up to date with the discussion at a glance.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle10](https://help.webex.com/images/video-ten-home.png)
Respond with confidence using Real-time assist
![Product icon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Customer Experience
Real-time assist is a Cisco AI Assistant feature designed to support you during live customer interactions by continuously understanding the conversation context, identifying customer intent, and instantly providing relevant recommendations. It guides you in real time by suggesting what to say or do, helping you navigate complex procedures, and even initiating workflows automatically.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)

### Video resources and tutorials
![](https://help.webex.com/images/view-thumbnail.svg)Show more
Check out admin video resources and tutorials
![](https://help.webex.com/images/view-thumbnail.svg)Show more
![Scroll left to see more video resources](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)![Scroll right to see more video resources](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneFirstVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-one.png)
Add users manually in Control Hub
You can manually add users at a time your organization by entering their email addresses. You can also manage external users who are in different organizations already by assigning a Webex Meetings license to them.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneSecondVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-two.png)
Delete a user from your organization in Control Hub
As a full administrator, you can delete a user if, for example, a user leaves your company or you made a typo in an email address that you entered when adding a new user.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneThirdVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-three.png)
Management Overview | Cisco Headsets and Desk Cameras
Explore six ways to manage Cisco Headsets and Desk Cameras, including the Webex App, Cisco Accessory Hub, Mobile Apps, and Control Hub, for seamless customization and control.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneFourthVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-four.png)
Assign organization account roles in Control Hub
You can set up users in your organization with different administrator roles. Users can become full administrators, or a combination of support administrators, user and device administrators, device administrators, read-only administrators, or compliance officers. You can also see a list of all or specific administrators in Control Hub, including device administrators and compliance officers.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneFifthVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-five.png)
Edit service licenses for individual users in Control Hub
When you invite people to your organization from Control Hub, they get a link to get started. If they haven't signed up after two days, they'll automatically get a reminder email. You can manually resend the invitation at any time.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneSixthVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-six.png)
Add multiple users in Control Hub with the CSV template
The CSV template allows you to add up to 20,000 users to your organization, and assign services at the same time.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneSeventhVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-seven.png)
Resend an invitation email from Control Hub
When you invite people to your organization from Control Hub, they get a link to get started. If they haven't signed up after two days, they'll automatically get a reminder email. You can manually resend the invitation at any time.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![enhancedAdmin.tabOneFirstVideoTitle](https://help.webex.com/images/suite-one.png)

# Webex AI
Webex delivers AI innovation across audio, video, and text so whether you’re supporting customers, collaborating with teams, or getting work done, you’re always at your best.
![Webex AI Agent icon](https://help.webex.com/images/bot-customer-assistant.svg)
#### Webex AI Agent
![](https://help.webex.com/images/arrow-down.svg)Learn more![](https://help.webex.com/images/arrow-down.svg)

#### Cisco AI Assistant

## Webex AI Agent
The new Webex AI Agent delivers a true self-service experience by combining conversational intelligence across voice and digital channels with real-time automation for more authentic, human-like interactions.

## Cisco AI Assistant
Just ask the AI Assistant. It boosts productivity for all teams and roles with AI-driven summaries, action items, responses, translations, search, and more.

Webex Suite
Customer Experience

Webex Instant Connect
![list-menu](https://help.webex.com/images/feedback.svg)Feedback?
Webex Instant Connect allows healthcare staff and patients to connect using simple, easy-to-use video telehealth consultations, scheduled directly from their Electronic Medical Records (EMR) portal. These browser-based consultations are highly secure, and no private health information is collected at any point. There is nothing for patients to install nor are there any login details to remember: the patient simply clicks the provided link. As a fully flexible platform, Webex Instant Connect now powers other healthcare use cases as well, such as in-patient teleconsultation, hourly virtual rounding, family visitation, and others. The general flow of Instant Connect can be used for any B2C meetings across industries like financial services, retail, real estate, government and much more. 
![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/464001-465000/464513.jpg)
Patients are able to view their upcoming appointments in their EMR portal and click on a link that starts their browser-based telehealth visit. While they’re waiting for the healthcare professional to join, patients can test their video, audio and speaker hardware. The healthcare professional also just needs to click the link provided, and they are instantly connected with the patient.
Visit [instant.webex.com](https://instant.webex.com/) to get started with Webex Instant Connect today!
[Webex Virtual Rounding](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/sv0h2ab/Webex-Instant-Connect#reference-template_a7caa81a-f172-448d-82c7-323b4a91df98)
The Webex Virtual Rounding solution builds on the flexibility of Webex Instant Connect to drive hourly rounding and in-patient virtual consultation use cases. Hourly rounding patients can be scheduled directly in the Electronic Medical Records (EMR) portal, and the provider just needs to go to the EMR portal’s patient context to launch the visit. The Cisco device facing the patient will join the call directly and the providers will be able to control the device’s camera, thus, simulating a physical visit.
**Advantages**
  1. Providers can join from anywhere using their laptop/mobile.
  2. Through Far End Camera Control (FECC), the providers can maneuver the patient’s device camera so that they can focus on the right area for diagnosis, essentially simulating a physical visit.
  3. Reduces Provider burden and increases overall efficiency resulting in millions of dollars of operational cost savings.


**Things to Note**
  1. We currently provide native integration with Epic EMR for this capability, but any EMR or healthcare system can integrate with Webex Virtual Rounding to unlock this workflow.
  2. The patient-to-device mapping must be maintained by the EMR or healthcare system.
  3. We currently support a no-knock scenario where the patient is notified on device that they will join the call in 15 seconds.
  4. Devices can be configured for this workflow by following the steps mentioned [here](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/6vsdoi/Configure-Virtual-Rounding).
  5. FECC and the supported devices list can be found [here](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nuwutmx/Control-remote-cameras-with-Instant-Connect).


**High-Level Workflow for Virtual Rounding – Epic Integration**
The following diagram shows a high-level example integration between Instant Connect Virtual Rounding and a typical Epic EMR installation. 
![Image described in the surrounding text.](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/470001-480000/472001-473000/472917.png)
[Browser requirements](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/sv0h2ab/Webex-Instant-Connect#Cisco_Reference.dita_deecc929-9801-4400-afed-1ba3936f1953)
Webex Instant Connect supports the following browsers:
  * **Mac OS**
    * Chrome version 75 and later
    * Firefox version 60 and later
    * Safari version 10 and later
  * **Windows OS**
    * Chrome version 75 and later
    * Firefox version 60 and later
    * Microsoft Edge
  * **iOS version 13 and later**
    * Safari version 10 and later
Chrome, Firefox, and Microsoft Edge are not supported on iOS.
Webex Instant Connect is not supported with iOS 15.1 or Safari 15.1. 
  * **Android**
    * Chrome version 75 and later
    * Firefox version 60 and later
    * Microsoft Edge


[Test your media](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/sv0h2ab/Webex-Instant-Connect#task-template_b328a9c0-e063-41b7-8ebf-c8286d348c2a)
To ensure you're ready for your consultation, test the following functions before joining:
  * OS compatibility
  * Internet speed
  * Microphone
  * Speaker
  * Camera

  
| 1  |  Go to <https://instant.webex.com/mediatest> and then click Start test. Webex Instant Connect starts testing each function.  |  
| --- | --- |  
| 2  |  Test your microphone by speaking, and then click Yes or No depending on the result. Click Skip if you don't want to test your microphone.  |  
| 3  |  Click Test to test your speaker, and then click Yes or No depending on the result. Click Skip if you don't want to test your speaker.  |  
| 4  |  During the video test, click Yes or No to indicate if your video preview appeared correctly or not.  |  
What to do next
Review the results of your media test, and correct any issues before you join your consultation.
[Meet with your patient](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/sv0h2ab/Webex-Instant-Connect#Cisco_Task.dita_5892c411-1a54-4429-a3e9-64bbd28d5f83)
Once you open your meeting link, the consultation lobby opens in your browser. Before you connect with your patient, you can sign in, open the consultation in Webex App, test your video, audio and speaker to ensure everything is working.
You can meet with more than just one patient. Webex Instant Connect supports up to 100 participants for each consultation.  
| 1  |  To sign in and use the capabilities of Webex App during your consultation, click Log In with Webex and sign in using your credentials. If you're happy to use your web browser and you don't need to sign in, click Join as Guest.  |  
| --- | --- |  
| 2  |  Click Continue in browser to host the consultation in your web browser, or click Launch in Webex App to open the consultation in Webex App.  |  
| 3  |  When you're ready to start the consultation with the patient, click Start Consultation. The consultation starts and your patient is added to the meeting.  |  
| 4  |  To invite more participants to the consultation using email, click ![invite guests](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/467001-468000/467895.jpg)Invite guests, enter their email address, click Confirm Invite and then click Send Invite.  |  
| 5  |  Use the following meeting controls during your consultation to ensure the patient has the best possible experience during their virtual visit. Most of these controls are also available to the patient, unless indicated otherwise.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/420001-430000/428001-429000/428102.jpg) – mute or unmute your microphone.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/430001-440000/438001-439000/438062.jpg) – turn your video on or off.
  * ![Share screen button](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/410001-420000/419001-420000/419427.jpg) – share your screen with the other participants.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/464001-465000/464871.jpg) (provider only) – dial out to an external number to add another participant, an interpreter for example, to the consultation. Enter the number and then click Call. Use the Keypad if you need to enter digits while on the call, and click End phone call when you're finished. This feature is not enabled by default. You must also be signed in to Webex and have the capability to make PSTN calls to use this feature.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/464001-465000/464514.jpg) – change the video layout, especially if there are more than 2 participants on the consultation. Choose from Single, Prominent or Equal.
  * ![Leave meeting or end meeting for everyone](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/420001-430000/428001-429000/428486.jpg) – providers can choose to end the consultation for all participants, or they can leave the consultation and rejoin it at a later time using the same link. If a patient clicks on this, they leave the consultation but it continues until the provider ends it. Patients can also rejoin the consultation at a later time using the same link
  * ![connect to device](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/467001-468000/467894.jpg) – signed in providers can connect to and transfer the consultation to their Webex device. Search for your device and then click Join. 
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/420001-430000/428001-429000/428490.jpg) – opens the participants panel, where providers can view, mute or remove participants. To remove a participant, click the ellipsis (…) beside the participants name, click Remove from meeting and then click Remove to confirm.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/420001-430000/428001-429000/428488.jpg) – opens the chat panel, where you can send messages to the other participants. The chat panel is also available in the consultation lobby. Messages are visible to everyone.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/464001-465000/464517.jpg) (provider only) – you can copy the technical details of the meeting to report a problem.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/464001-465000/464516.jpg) (provider only) – see meeting information such as dial-in numbers and meeting links.
  * ![Network connection and CPU usage indicator](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/465001-466000/465837.jpg) – indicates the quality of your network connection. If your network connection drops, a warning message appears to let you know about the problem and the actions you can take to solve it.
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/460001-470000/464001-465000/464515.jpg) – choose which camera and microphone you'd like to use during the consultation. You can also choose to enable blurred background or disable background noise reduction for the meeting. 
  * ![](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/470001-480000/470001-471000/470355.jpg) – Enable or disable picture-in-picture mode.
  * ![Remote camera available icon.](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/470001-480000/472001-473000/472034.jpg) - Access a Far End Camera Control (FECC) enabled device.

 |  
| 6  |  The provider can submit feedback on the audio and video quality after the consultation ends. Select a star rating and click Submit Feedback. Click Skip if you don't want to provide feedback.  |  
[Instant Connect Integration](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/sv0h2ab/Webex-Instant-Connect#reference-template_2e099afb-818c-4283-b4ae-f15bb985470b)
See the following sections to get started integrating Instant Connect with your own system. If you encounter any issues, please contact your Cisco AM.
### Integrate with Epic for Telehealth
To integrate Instant Connect with Epic for Telehealth:
  1. Go to [https://instant.webex.com](https://instant.webex.com/) and select the **Epic Integration Page**.
  2. Select **Begin Integration with Epic** and then login as Webex administrator for your organization.
  3. Select **Generate Secret Key** , and save the **Secret Key** , **URL** , and **Parameters** entries to a temporary location.
  4. Login to your Epic environment and paste the secret key into the **CryptKey** field, the URL into the **CryptURL** field, and the parameters into the **PatientOpenURL** field in the Epic configuration page within your FDI records for each Epic application you intend to use.
  5. Create a **Device Integration** for each Epic application you intend to use.
  6. Create an **Appointment Type** within Epic.
  7. Make sure access to the domain, `instant.webex.com,` is enabled in your Epic configuration.


This is a one-time configuration process.
### Integrate with Epic for Virtual Rounding (device integration)
To integrate Instant Connect with Epic for Virtual Rounding (device integration):
  1. Go to [https://instant.webex.com](https://instant.webex.com/) and select the **Epic Integration Page**.
  2. Select **Begin Integration with Epic** and then login as Webex administrator for your organization.
  3. Select **Generate Secret Key** , and save the **Secret Key** , **URL** , entries to a temporary location. 
The Provider Parameters entry for this integration is: `visitID=%CSN%&userType=1&userID=%EPICUSERID;;; ;                 ;;NONE;%&flow={"id":"sip-no-knock","data":[{"uri":%EXTENSION;101336%}]}` where `%EXTENSION;101336%` is the parameter which contains SIP address of the Webex Device that is in front of the patient.
  4. Login to your Epic environment and paste the secret key into the **CryptKey** field, the URL into the **CryptURL** field, and the parameters into the **PatientOpenURL** field in the Epic configuration page within your FDI records for each Epic application you intend to use. You’ll only need to configure the **Provider Record** in this case.
  5. Create a **Device Integration** for each Epic application you intend to use.
  6. Create an **Appointment Type** within Epic.
  7. Make sure access to the domain, `instant.webex.com,` is enabled in your Epic configuration.
  8. A Webex administrator from your customer organization needs to set up Webex Devices in Control Hub to work with this flow. See [Configure Virtual Rounding](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/6vsdoi/Configure-Virtual-Rounding).
  9. Most new Webex devices also support Far End Camera Control with this workflow. Based on your devices and your Room OS version, some configurations might need to be updated in Control Hub. See [Control remote cameras with Instant Connect](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nuwutmx/Control-remote-cameras-with-Instant-Connect).


This is a one-time configuration process.
### Integrate other systems with Instant Connect
To integrate Instant Connect with other systems:
  1. Go to [https://instant.webex.com](https://instant.webex.com/).
  2. Select **Begin Integration** and then login as Webex administrator for your organization.
  3. Select **Activate Instant Connect**.
  4. Go to this Webex Developer Portal [link](https://developer.webex.com/docs/instant-connect-meeting-links) and follow the steps to integrate Webex Instant Connect with your EHR or any other clinical healthcare system. 
You’ll need to add `verticalType=”gen”` (for generic) in the API for non-healthcare workflows. The default vertical is `hc` (healthcare).
  5. Once complete, your customers can leverage Webex Instant Connect links constructed by the endpoint in any way they desire.
  6. Make sure access to the domain, `instant.webex.com,` is enabled to avoid firewall issues.


This is a one-time configuration process.
### Integrate other systems with the Instant Connect device integration workflow
To integrate other systems with the Instant Connect device integration workflow:
  1. Go to [https://instant.webex.com](https://instant.webex.com/).
  2. Select **Begin Integration** and then login as Webex administrator for your organization.
  3. Select **Activate Instant Connect**.
  4. Go to this Webex Developer Portal [link](https://developer.webex.com/docs/instant-connect-meeting-links) and follow the steps to integrate Webex Instant Connect with your EHR or any other clinical healthcare system.
  5. Follow the section **Complete Sample JSON Request Payload Including Device Support** to understand parameters required for integrating Webex Device with your workflow. 
**NOTE** : You’ll need to add `verticalType=”gen”` (for generic) in the API for non-healthcare workflows. The default vertical is `hc` (healthcare).
  6. A Webex administrator from your customer organization needs to set up Webex Devices in Control Hub to work with this flow. See [Configure Virtual Rounding](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/6vsdoi/Configure-Virtual-Rounding).
  7. Most new Webex devices also support Far End Camera Control with this workflow. Based on your devices and your Room OS version, some configurations might need to be updated in Control Hub. See [Control remote cameras with Instant Connect](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/nuwutmx/Control-remote-cameras-with-Instant-Connect).
  8. Once complete, your customers can leverage Webex Instant Connect links constructed by the endpoint in any way they desire.
  9. Make sure access to the domain, `instant.webex.com,` is enabled to avoid firewall issues.


This is a one-time configuration process.
[Manage phone book contacts ](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/sv0h2ab/Webex-Instant-Connect#reference-template_25159e56-b4c7-496b-9cfe-c47608da14e8)
Webex Instant Connect now allows administrators to add and edit contacts in the phone book. The contacts are then displayed in the Instant Connect phone book for Instant Connect users.
To add customized contacts to your Instant Connect phonebook:
  1. Login to your administrator account on [https://instant.webex.com](https://instant.webex.com/).
  2. Choose **Feature customization** in the left pane and, make sure **Dialout** is enabled, and then enable **Phonebook**. Use **Add Contact** to add a new contact and the pencil and trash icons to modify or remove existing contacts: 
![Image is described in the surrounding text.](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/470001-480000/479001-480000/479927.png)
  3. The list of contacts will be displayed to users in the Instant Connect dialer: 
![Image is described in the surrounding text.](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/470001-480000/479001-480000/479928.png)


[Disable screen sharing](https://help.webex.com/en-us/article/sv0h2ab/Webex-Instant-Connect#reference-template_b5c4c48c-a057-48ce-924c-73fac41637ea)
To improve the customer experience, Instant Connect offers an option to disable screen sharing.
To disable screen sharing:
  1. Login to your administrator account on [https://instant.webex.com](https://instant.webex.com/).
  2. Choose **Feature customization** in the left pane and enable or disable **Screen Share** as you require: 
![Image described in the surrounding text.](https://cisco-api.ingeniuxondemand.com/DITA/content/en/us/td/i/400001-500000/470001-480000/479001-480000/479929.png)

![Rul til venstre for at se flere videoressourcer](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)![Rul til højre for at se flere videoressourcer](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Cisco AI Assistant i møder
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Nogle gange kommer du for sent til et møde, du har måske brug for at forlade et møde et øjeblik, eller du laver andre opgaver under et møde og bliver distraheret. Med AI Assistant-mødeoversigten kan du hurtigt holde dig opdateret omkring de ting, du er gået glip af, forstå og følge samtalen og stille spørgsmål uden at forstyrre mødet. Du modtager også en oversigt og en afskrift efter mødet, uden at du behøver at optage.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Opret en autonom AI-agent
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Autonome AI-agenter opererer uafhængigt uden direkte menneskelig indgriben. Autonome agenter kan få adgang til og anvende et videnslager for at tilvejebringe informative og korrekte svar på brugerforespørgsler. Disse agenter bruger avancerede algoritmer og maskinlæringsteknikker til at analysere data, lære af deres miljø og tilpasse deres handlinger for at opnå specifikke mål.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Del indhold i et møde
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Når du deler indhold i Webex-appen, kan alle i mødet nemt se, hvad der tales om. Alle kan dele indhold, men kun én person kan dele ad gangen.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Opnå større effektivitet ved hjælp af AI-genererede oversigter
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Ciscos AI Assistant tilbyder præcise oversigter over tidligere interaktioner, så agenter kan fortsætte samtaler ubesværet uden behov for at indsamle oplysninger igen. Denne øjeblikkelige adgang giver agenter mulighed for at fokusere på at levere effektive løsninger, hvilket fører til hurtigere løsninger og en mere sammenhængende kundeoplevelse.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
Webex-app | Tilføj dit profilbillede
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Tilføj et billede til din profil for at gøre det nemmere for andre at genkende dig i Webex-appen. Indtil du overfører et billede, viser din profil kun dine initialer i en cirkel.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Beskeder om trivselspauser for AI Assistant
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Når AI Assistant anbefaler en trivselspause, vises en besked: Accept af en pause: Klik på Hold en pause for at acceptere pausen. Cisco AI Assistant-widgetten åbner automatisk for at bekræfte dit valg. Når du har afsluttet den aktuelle interaktion, starter din trivselspause på ét minut automatisk. Din status ændrer sig til Trivselspause for at forhindre, at den næste interaktion kommer i kø til dig. En nedtælling på fem sekunder vises på skærmen, når din pause på ét minut er færdig. Når den er færdig, lukker pausevinduet automatisk, og din status ændrer sig til Tilgængelig, så du er klar til den næste interaktion.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)
Generér rumoversigter
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Når du har travlt, eller du har været væk fra kontoret, kan det være en udfordring at følge med i alle dine rum. AI Assistant kan generere rumoversigter, så du hurtigt kan få oplysninger om ubesvarede meddelelser og samtaler i rummet. Hold dig underrettet om beslutninger og vigtige punkter, så du kan følge med i samtalen med det samme.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle8](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Få bedre effektivitet med udskifter i realtid
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Udskrifter i realtid er udviklet til at hjælpe dig med at optimere effektiviteten af kommunikation under kundeinteraktioner. Ved at levere livetranscriptioner direkte på din Agent Desktop kan det hjælpe med at forbedre fokus, klarhed og svarnøjagtighed. Den er udviklet til at forbedre din forståelse og effektivitet under opkald, mindske misforståelser og støtte dig i at levere interaktioner af høj kvalitet, der forbedrer præstationen og kundetilfredsheden.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle9](https://help.webex.com/images/video-nine-home.png)
Brug whiteboardet i møder
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Når du har travlt, eller du har været væk fra kontoret, kan det være en udfordring at følge med i alle dine rum. AI Assistant kan generere rumoversigter, så du hurtigt kan få oplysninger om ubesvarede meddelelser og samtaler i rummet. Hold dig underrettet om beslutninger og vigtige punkter, så du kan følge med i samtalen med det samme.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle10](https://help.webex.com/images/video-ten-home.png)
Levér selvsikre svar med hjælp i realtid
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Hjælp i realtid er en Cisco AI Assistant-funktion, der er udviklet til at støtte dig under livekundeinteraktioner ved løbende at sikre forståelse af samtalens kontekst, identificere kundehensigten og øjeblikkeligt levere relevante anbefalinger. Den vejleder dig i realtid ved at foreslå, hvad du skal sige eller gøre, og den hjælper dig med at navigere i komplekse procedurer og endda starte arbejdsgange automatisk.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Cisco AI Assistant i møder
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Nogle gange kommer du for sent til et møde, du har måske brug for at forlade et møde et øjeblik, eller du laver andre opgaver under et møde og bliver distraheret. Med AI Assistant-mødeoversigten kan du hurtigt holde dig opdateret omkring de ting, du er gået glip af, forstå og følge samtalen og stille spørgsmål uden at forstyrre mødet. Du modtager også en oversigt og en afskrift efter mødet, uden at du behøver at optage.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Opret en autonom AI-agent
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Autonome AI-agenter opererer uafhængigt uden direkte menneskelig indgriben. Autonome agenter kan få adgang til og anvende et videnslager for at tilvejebringe informative og korrekte svar på brugerforespørgsler. Disse agenter bruger avancerede algoritmer og maskinlæringsteknikker til at analysere data, lære af deres miljø og tilpasse deres handlinger for at opnå specifikke mål.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Del indhold i et møde
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Når du deler indhold i Webex-appen, kan alle i mødet nemt se, hvad der tales om. Alle kan dele indhold, men kun én person kan dele ad gangen.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Opnå større effektivitet ved hjælp af AI-genererede oversigter
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Ciscos AI Assistant tilbyder præcise oversigter over tidligere interaktioner, så agenter kan fortsætte samtaler ubesværet uden behov for at indsamle oplysninger igen. Denne øjeblikkelige adgang giver agenter mulighed for at fokusere på at levere effektive løsninger, hvilket fører til hurtigere løsninger og en mere sammenhængende kundeoplevelse.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
Webex-app | Tilføj dit profilbillede
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Tilføj et billede til din profil for at gøre det nemmere for andre at genkende dig i Webex-appen. Indtil du overfører et billede, viser din profil kun dine initialer i en cirkel.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Beskeder om trivselspauser for AI Assistant
![Produktikon](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundeoplevelse
Når AI Assistant anbefaler en trivselspause, vises en besked: Accept af en pause: Klik på Hold en pause for at acceptere pausen. Cisco AI Assistant-widgetten åbner automatisk for at bekræfte dit valg. Når du har afsluttet den aktuelle interaktion, starter din trivselspause på ét minut automatisk. Din status ændrer sig til Trivselspause for at forhindre, at den næste interaktion kommer i kø til dig. En nedtælling på fem sekunder vises på skærmen, når din pause på ét minut er færdig. Når den er færdig, lukker pausevinduet automatisk, og din status ændrer sig til Tilgængelig, så du er klar til den næste interaktion.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)

![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)

Cisco AI Assistant in meetings
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Manchmal treten Sie Meeting verspätet bei, müssen kurz weggehen oder sind durch Multitasking abgelenkt. Durch die Meeting-Zusammenfassungen des KI-Assistenten können Sie sich schnelle darüber informieren, was Sie verpasst haben, der Diskussion folgen und Fragen stellen, ohne das Meeting zu stören, oder bei der Unterhaltung auf dem Laufenden bleiben. Außerdem erhalten Sie nach dem Meeting eine Zusammenfassung und ein Transkript, ohne das Sie es aufzeichnen müssen.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Autonomen KI-Agenten erstellen
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundenerfahrung
Autonome KI-Agenten arbeiten unabhängig und ohne direkten Eingriff von einem Menschen. Autonome KI-Agenten können auf eine Wissensdatenbank zugreifen und diese nutzen, um informative und genaue Antworten auf Benutzeranfragen zu geben. Diese Agenten nutzen fortschrittliche Algorithmen und Techniken des maschinellen Lernens, um Daten zu analysieren, aus ihrer Umgebung zu lernen und ihr Verhalten anzupassen, um bestimmte Ziele zu erreichen.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Freigeben von Inhalten während eines Meetings
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Wenn Sie Inhalte in der Webex-App freigeben, können alle Meeting-Teilnehmer leicht sehen, was gerade besprochen wird. Jeder kann Inhalte freigeben, es kann jedoch jeweils nur ein Bildschirm freigegeben werden.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Effizienzsteigerung mit KI-generierten Zusammenfassungen
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundenerfahrung
Der Cisco AI Assistant bietet prägnante Zusammenfassungen früherer Interaktionen, sodass die Agenten Gespräche nahtlos fortsetzen können, ohne Informationen erneut einholen zu müssen. Durch diesen sofortigen Zugriff können sich die Agenten darauf konzentrieren, effektive Lösungen anzubieten, was zu einer schnelleren Problemlösung und einem einheitlicheren Kundenerlebnis führt.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
Webex-App | Hinzufügen Ihres Profilbilds
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Erleichtern Sie es anderen, Sie in de Webex-App zu erkennen, indem Sie Ihrem Profil ein Foto hinzufügen. Solange Sie kein Foto hochgeladen haben, werden als Profilfoto nur Ihre Initialen in einem Kreis angezeigt.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Benachrichtigungen des AI Assistant zu Erholungspausen
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundenerfahrung
Wenn der AI Assistant eine Erholungspause empfiehlt, erscheint eine Toaster-Benachrichtigung: Eine Pause annehmen: Klicken Sie auf die Schaltfläche für eine Pause, um die Pause anzunehmen. Das Cisco AI Assistant-Widget öffnet sich automatisch, um Ihre Auswahl zu bestätigen. Nachdem Sie die aktuelle Interaktion beendet haben, beginnt automatisch Ihre 1-minütige Erholungspause. Ihr Status ändert sich zu „Erholungspause“, damit Ihnen nicht die nächste Interaktion zugewiesen wird. Wenn Ihre 1-minütige Pause zu Ende geht, erscheint ein 5-Sekunden-Countdown auf dem Bildschirm. Sobald die Pause beendet ist, schließt sich das Pausenfenster automatisch und Ihr Status ändert sich zu „Verfügbar“, sodass Sie für die nächste Interaktion bereitstehen.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)
Zusammenfassungen von Bereichen erstellen
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Wenn Sie beschäftig sind oder einige Zeit nicht im Büro waren, kann es mühsam sein, in allen Bereich wieder auf den neuesten Stand zu kommen. Der KI-Assistent kann Bereichszusammenfassungen generieren, damit Sie schnell einen Überblick über alle verpassten Nachrichten und Unterhaltungen im Bereich erhalten und wieder auf dem Laufenden sind. Bleiben Sie informiert, was Entscheidungen und wichtige Punkte betrifft, und sehen Sie auf einen Blick, wo die Diskussion gerade steht.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle8](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Höhere Effizienz mit Abschriften in Echtzeit
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundenerfahrung
Abschriften in Echtzeit sollen Ihnen dabei helfen, Ihre Kommunikationseffizienz während Interaktionen mit Kunden zu optimieren. Durch die Bereitstellung von Live-Transkripten direkt auf Ihrem Agent Desktop können Sie Ihre Konzentration sowie die Klarheit und die Genauigkeit Ihrer Antworten verbessern. Das Ziel besteht darin, Ihr Verständnis und Ihre Effizienz bei Telefonaten zu verbessern, Missverständnisse zu vermeiden und Sie dabei zu unterstützen, hochwertige Interaktionen zu liefern, die die Leistung und die Kundenzufriedenheit steigern.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle9](https://help.webex.com/images/video-nine-home.png)
Whiteboard in Meetings verwenden
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite

Der KI-Assistent kann Bereichszusammenfassungen generieren, damit Sie schnell einen Überblick über alle verpassten Nachrichten und Unterhaltungen im Bereich erhalten und wieder auf dem Laufenden sind. Bleiben Sie informiert, was Entscheidungen und wichtige Punkte betrifft, und sehen Sie auf einen Blick, wo die Diskussion gerade steht.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle10](https://help.webex.com/images/video-ten-home.png)
Mit der Echtzeitunterstützung souverän antworten
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Kundenerfahrung
Die Echtzeitunterstützung ist eine Funktion von Cisco AI Assistant, die Sie während Live-Kundeninteraktionen unterstützt, indem sie kontinuierlich den Gesprächskontext erfasst, die Absicht des Kunden erkennt und sofort relevante Empfehlungen gibt. Sie unterstützt Sie in Echtzeit, indem sie Ihnen Vorschläge macht, was Sie sagen oder tun sollen, Ihnen hilft, komplexe Abläufe zu bewältigen, und sogar automatisch Arbeitsabläufe beginnt.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Cisco AI Assistant in meetings
![Produktbereich](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)

## Andere Ressourcen
![Webex herunterladen](https://help.webex.com/images/webex-symbol.svg)
Webex herunterladen
Eine Plattform mit allen Vernetzungsoptionen:
![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)
![Webex-Community](https://help.webex.com/images/webexCommunity.svg)
Webex-Community
Die Community ist ein Hub, auf dem Sie sich mit Ihren Peers vernetzen und Cisco-Spezialisten um Hilfe bitten, Ihr Fachwissen teilen, ein Netzwerk aufbauen und sich beruflich weiterentwickeln können.
![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)
![Webex Insider](https://help.webex.com/images/webexInsider.svg)
Webex Insider
Vernetzen Sie sich mit anderen Personen, die Ihre Leidenschaft für Webex teilen, und erhalten Sie Insiderzugang zu den neuen Konzepten der Webex-Produktportfolio- und -Erfahrungsteams.
![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)
![Webex für Entwickler:](https://help.webex.com/images/webexDevelopers.svg)
Webex für Entwickler:
Entwickeln Sie die Zukunft der geschäftlichen Zusammenarbeit mit Webex. Erfahren Sie mehr über die Webex API und SDKs, um die nächste großartige Zusammenarbeits-App auf der Webex-Plattform zu erstellen.
![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)
![Benutzer trägt ein Headset-Symbol](https://help.webex.com/images/needHelpImg.svg)

![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Cisco AI Assistant en reuniones
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
A veces entra tarde a una reunión, debe ausentarse un momento o realiza varias tareas a la vez y se distrae. Con los resúmenes de reuniones generados por el AI Assistant, puede ponerse al corriente rápidamente de lo que se ha perdido, entender el debate, hacer preguntas sin interrumpir la reunión y seguir al tanto de la conversación. También recibe un resumen y una transcripción después de la reunión, sin tener que grabar.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Crear un agente autónomo de IA
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Experiencia del cliente
Los agentes autónomos de IA operan de manera independiente, sin intervención humana directa. Los agentes autónomos pueden acceder a un repositorio de conocimientos y utilizarlo para proporcionar respuestas informativas y precisas a las consultas de los usuarios. Estos agentes usan algoritmos avanzados y técnicas de aprendizaje automático para analizar datos, aprender de su entorno y adaptar sus acciones para alcanzar objetivos específicos.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Compartir contenido en una reunión
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Cuando comparte contenido en la aplicación de Webex, todos los participantes de la reunión pueden ver fácilmente qué se está discutiendo. Cualquiera puede compartir contenido, pero solo una persona puede compartir por vez.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Aumente la eficacia mediante resúmenes generados por la IA
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Experiencia del cliente
Cisco AI Assistant ofrece resúmenes concisos de las interacciones previas, lo que permite a los agentes continuar las conversaciones de forma fluida y sin tener que volver a recopilar información. Este acceso inmediato posibilita que los agentes se enfoquen en proporcionar soluciones efectivas, lo que conduce a resoluciones más rápidas y una experiencia del cliente más cohesiva.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
Aplicación de Webex | Agregar su imagen de perfil
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Agregue una imagen a su perfil para que a los demás les resulte más fácil reconocerlo en la aplicación de Webex. Hasta que cargue una, su perfil solo mostrará sus iniciales dentro de un círculo.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Notificaciones de AI Assistant sobre descansos para el bienestar
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Experiencia del cliente
Cuando AI Assistant recomiende un descanso para el bienestar, aparecerá una notificación emergente: Aceptación del descanso: Haga clic en “Tomar un descanso” para aceptar la recomendación. El widget de Cisco AI Assistant se abrirá automáticamente para confirmar su elección. Una vez que concluya la interacción actual, comenzará su descanso para el bienestar de 1 minuto de manera automática. Su estado cambiará a “Descanso para el bienestar” para que la siguiente interacción no se agregue a su cola. Cuando su descanso de 1 minuto esté por terminar, aparecerá en pantalla una cuenta regresiva de 5 segundos. Al finalizar, la ventana de descanso se cerrará automáticamente, y su estado cambiará a “Disponible”, con lo que quedará listo para la siguiente interacción.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)
Genere resúmenes de espacio
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Cuando está ocupado o ha estado fuera de la oficina, ponerse al día con todos sus espacios puede ser complicado. El AI Assistant puede generar resúmenes de espacios para ayudarlo a ponerse al día rápidamente sobre los mensajes y conversaciones que se haya perdido en el espacio. Manténgase informado sobre las decisiones y los puntos clave y póngase al día con el debate de un vistazo.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle8](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Aumente la eficacia con transcripciones en tiempo real
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Experiencia del cliente
Las transcripciones en tiempo real están diseñadas para ayudarle a optimizar la eficiencia de la comunicación durante las interacciones con los clientes. Las transcripciones en vivo directamente en su Agent Desktop pueden ayudar a mejorar el foco, la claridad y la precisión de las respuestas. Esto apunta a mejorar la comprensión y la eficiencia durante las llamadas, reducir los malentendidos y ayudar a que el usuario tenga interacciones de alta calidad que incrementen su rendimiento y la satisfacción de los clientes.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle9](https://help.webex.com/images/video-nine-home.png)
Utilice la pizarra blanca en las reuniones
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Cuando está ocupado o ha estado fuera de la oficina, ponerse al día con todos sus espacios puede ser complicado. El AI Assistant puede generar resúmenes de espacios para ayudarlo a ponerse al día rápidamente sobre los mensajes y conversaciones que se haya perdido en el espacio. Manténgase informado sobre las decisiones y los puntos clave y póngase al día con el debate de un vistazo.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle10](https://help.webex.com/images/video-ten-home.png)
Responda con confianza utilizando la asistencia en tiempo real
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Experiencia del cliente
La asistencia en tiempo real es una función de Cisco AI Assistant diseñada para ayudarle durante las interacciones con clientes en vivo. Esta función le permite comprender de manera continua el contexto de la conversación, identificar la intención del cliente y ofrecer al instante recomendaciones relevantes. Le proporciona una guía en tiempo real, ya que le sugiere qué hacer o decir, le ayudar a navegar procedimientos complejos e incluso a iniciar flujos de trabajo de manera automática.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)

Utilice la pizarra blanca en las reuniones
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Cuando está ocupado o ha estado fuera de la oficina, ponerse al día con todos sus espacios puede ser complicado. El AI Assistant puede generar resúmenes de espacios para ayudarlo a ponerse al día rápidamente sobre los mensajes y conversaciones que se haya perdido en el espacio. Manténgase informado sobre las decisiones y los puntos clave y póngase al día con el debate de un vistazo.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle10](https://help.webex.com/images/video-ten-home.png)
Responda con confianza utilizando la asistencia en tiempo real
![Icono de producto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Experiencia del cliente
La asistencia en tiempo real es una función de Cisco AI Assistant diseñada para ayudarle durante las interacciones con clientes en vivo. Esta función le permite comprender de manera continua el contexto de la conversación, identificar la intención del cliente y ofrecer al instante recomendaciones relevantes. Le proporciona una guía en tiempo real, ya que le sugiere qué hacer o decir, le ayudar a navegar procedimientos complejos e incluso a iniciar flujos de trabajo de manera automática.
Descubrir más recursos de video

![Défiler vers la gauche pour voir plus de ressources vidéo](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)![Défiler vers la droite pour voir plus de ressources vidéo](https://help.webex.com/images/video-arrow.svg)
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Assistant IA de Cisco au cours des réunions
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Il peut arriver que vous rejoigniez une réunion en retard, que vous ayez besoin de vous absenter un instant ou que vous soyez distrait en raison de vos activités multitâches. Grâce aux résumés de réunion de l’assistant IA, vous pouvez rapidement rattraper ce que vous avez manqué, comprendre la discussion, poser des questions sans perturber la réunion et rester au fait de la conversation. Vous recevez également un résumé et une transcription après la réunion, sans avoir à l’enregistrer.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Créer un agent d’IA autonome
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
Les agents IA autonomes fonctionnent de manière indépendante, sans intervention humaine directe. Les agents autonomes peuvent accéder à un référentiel de connaissances et l’utiliser pour fournir des réponses informatives et précises aux questions des utilisateurs. Ces agents utilisent des algorithmes avancés et des techniques d’apprentissage automatique pour analyser les données, tirer des enseignements de leur environnement et adapter leurs actions afin d’atteindre des objectifs précis.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Partager du contenu pendant une réunion
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Lorsque vous partagez du contenu dans l’application Webex, tous les participants à la réunion peuvent facilement voir les sujets de discussion. Tout le monde peut partager du contenu, mais une seule personne peut le faire à la fois.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Améliorer l’efficacité en utilisant les sommaires générés par l’IA
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
Cisco AI Assistant propose des sommaires concis des interactions précédentes, ce qui permet aux agents de reprendre facilement la conversation, sans avoir à recueillir à nouveau les informations. Cet accès immédiat permet aux agents de se concentrer sur la mise en place de solutions efficaces, ce qui se traduit par des résolutions plus rapides et une expérience client plus cohérente.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
Application Webex | Ajouter votre photo de profil
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Aidez les personnes à vous reconnaitre dans l’application Webex en ajoutant une photo à votre profil. Jusqu’à ce que vous en ayez chargé une, votre profil affiche uniquement vos initiales dans un cercle.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Notifications de pause détente par AI Assistant
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
Lorsqu’AI Assistant recommande une pause bien-être, une notification toaster s’affiche : Accepter une pause : Acceptez la pause en cliquant sur Faire une pause. Le widget Cisco AI Assistant s’ouvre automatiquement pour confirmer votre choix. Une fois votre interaction en cours terminée, votre pause bien-être d’une minute commence automatiquement. Votre statut passe à Pause bien-être, afin d’empêcher que la prochaine interaction ne vous soit attribuée. Un compte à rebours de 5 secondes s’affiche à l’écran à la fin de votre pause d’une minute. Une fois fini, la fenêtre de pause se ferme automatiquement et votre statut passe à Disponible, vous permettant ainsi d’être prêt pour la prochaine interaction.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)
Générer des résumés des espaces
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Lorsque vous êtes occupé ou que vous vous êtes absenté du bureau, il peut s’avérer difficile de rattraper tout ce qui s’est passé dans vos espaces. L’assistant IA peut générer des résumés d’espace pour vous aider à rattraper rapidement les messages et conversations manqués dans l’espace. Restez informé des décisions et des points forts, et restez au fait de la discussion en un coup d’œil.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle8](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Améliorer l’efficacité grâce aux transcriptions en temps réel
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
Les transcriptions en temps réel sont conçues pour vous aider à optimiser l’efficacité de la communication lors de vos interactions avec les clients. En proposant des transcriptions en temps réel directement sur votre Agent Desktop, cette fonctionnalité peut vous aider à améliorer votre concentration, votre clarté et la précision de vos réponses. Elle vise à améliorer votre compréhension et votre efficacité lors des appels, à réduire les malentendus et à vous aider à offrir des interactions de grande qualité qui améliorent les performances et la satisfaction des clients.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle9](https://help.webex.com/images/video-nine-home.png)
Utilisation du tableau blanc en réunion
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Lorsque vous êtes occupé ou que vous vous êtes absenté du bureau, il peut s’avérer difficile de rattraper tout ce qui s’est passé dans vos espaces. L’assistant IA peut générer des résumés d’espace pour vous aider à rattraper rapidement les messages et conversations manqués dans l’espace. Restez informé des décisions et des points forts, et restez au fait de la discussion en un coup d’œil.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle10](https://help.webex.com/images/video-ten-home.png)
Répondre en toute confiance grâce à l’assistance en temps réel
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
L’assistance en temps réel est une fonctionnalité de Cisco AI Assistant conçue pour vous aider lors des interactions en direct avec les clients, en analysant en permanence le contexte de la conversation, en identifiant les intentions du client et en proposant instantanément des recommandations pertinentes. Elle vous guide en temps réel en vous suggérant quoi dire ou quoi faire, en vous aidant à naviguer les procédures complexes et même en lançant automatiquement des processus.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Assistant IA de Cisco au cours des réunions
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Il peut arriver que vous rejoigniez une réunion en retard, que vous ayez besoin de vous absenter un instant ou que vous soyez distrait en raison de vos activités multitâches. Grâce aux résumés de réunion de l’assistant IA, vous pouvez rapidement rattraper ce que vous avez manqué, comprendre la discussion, poser des questions sans perturber la réunion et rester au fait de la conversation. Vous recevez également un résumé et une transcription après la réunion, sans avoir à l’enregistrer.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Créer un agent d’IA autonome
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
Les agents IA autonomes fonctionnent de manière indépendante, sans intervention humaine directe. Les agents autonomes peuvent accéder à un référentiel de connaissances et l’utiliser pour fournir des réponses informatives et précises aux questions des utilisateurs. Ces agents utilisent des algorithmes avancés et des techniques d’apprentissage automatique pour analyser les données, tirer des enseignements de leur environnement et adapter leurs actions afin d’atteindre des objectifs précis.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Partager du contenu pendant une réunion
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Lorsque vous partagez du contenu dans l’application Webex, tous les participants à la réunion peuvent facilement voir les sujets de discussion. Tout le monde peut partager du contenu, mais une seule personne peut le faire à la fois.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Améliorer l’efficacité en utilisant les sommaires générés par l’IA
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
Cisco AI Assistant propose des sommaires concis des interactions précédentes, ce qui permet aux agents de reprendre facilement la conversation, sans avoir à recueillir à nouveau les informations. Cet accès immédiat permet aux agents de se concentrer sur la mise en place de solutions efficaces, ce qui se traduit par des résolutions plus rapides et une expérience client plus cohérente.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
Application Webex | Ajouter votre photo de profil
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Aidez les personnes à vous reconnaitre dans l’application Webex en ajoutant une photo à votre profil. Jusqu’à ce que vous en ayez chargé une, votre profil affiche uniquement vos initiales dans un cercle.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Notifications de pause détente par AI Assistant
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Expérience client
Lorsqu’AI Assistant recommande une pause bien-être, une notification toaster s’affiche : Accepter une pause : Acceptez la pause en cliquant sur Faire une pause. Le widget Cisco AI Assistant s’ouvre automatiquement pour confirmer votre choix. Une fois votre interaction en cours terminée, votre pause bien-être d’une minute commence automatiquement. Votre statut passe à Pause bien-être, afin d’empêcher que la prochaine interaction ne vous soit attribuée. Un compte à rebours de 5 secondes s’affiche à l’écran à la fin de votre pause d’une minute. Une fois fini, la fenêtre de pause se ferme automatiquement et votre statut passe à Disponible, vous permettant ainsi d’être prêt pour la prochaine interaction.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)
Générer des résumés des espaces
![Icône Produit](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)

![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)
Cisco AI Assistant nelle riunioni
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Talvolta, potresti arrivare in ritardo a una riunione, avere la necessità di allontanarti per un momento oppure essere occupato in altre attività ed essere distratto. Con i riepiloghi delle riunioni dell'AI Assistant, puoi facilmente rimanere aggiornato per capire cosa è stato discusso, porre domande senza disturbare la riunione e seguire la conversazione. Puoi anche ricevere un riepilogo e una trascrizione dopo la riunione senza la necessità di registrarla.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle2](https://help.webex.com/images/video-two-home.png)
Creazione di un agente AI autonomo
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Esperienza cliente
Gli agenti AI autonomi operano in modo indipendente senza intervento diretto dell'utente. Gli agenti autonomi possono accedere e usare un repository di conoscenze per fornire risposte informative e accurate alle domande degli utenti. Questi agenti usano algoritmi avanzati e tecniche di apprendimento automatico per analizzare i dati, apprendere dal relativo ambiente e adattare le proprie azioni per raggiungere obiettivi specifici.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle3](https://help.webex.com/images/video-three-home.png)
Condivisione di contenuto in una riunione
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Quando condividi contenuto nell'app Webex, tutti i partecipanti alla riunione possono facilmente vedere cosa viene discusso. Chiunque può condividere il contenuto, ma solo una persona alla volta può condividerlo.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle4](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Miglioramento dell'efficienza tramite riepiloghi generati da AI
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Esperienza cliente
Cisco AI Assistant offre riepiloghi concisi di interazioni precedenti, consentendo agli agenti di continuare le conversazioni senza interruzioni e senza dover raccogliere nuovamente informazioni. Questo accesso immediato consente agli agenti di concentrarsi sull'offerta di soluzioni efficienti, ottenendo risoluzioni più veloci e un'esperienza clienti più coesa.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle5](https://help.webex.com/images/video-five-home.png)
App Webex | Aggiunta della tua immagine di profilo
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Fai in modo che gli altri ti riconoscano facilmente nell'app Webex aggiungendo un'immagine al profilo. Finché non ne carichi una, il tuo profilo visualizza solo le tue iniziali all'interno di un cerchio.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle6](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Notifiche di pause benessere di AI Assistant
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Esperienza cliente
Quando AI Assistant consiglia una pausa benessere, viene visualizzata una notifica di avviso popup: Accettazione di una pausa: fai clic su Fai una pausa per accettare la pausa. Il widget Cisco AI Assistant si apre automaticamente per confermare la scelta. Dopo aver riepilogato l'interazione corrente, la tua pausa benessere di 1 minuto inizia automaticamente. Il tuo stato cambia in Pausa benessere per evitare che la successiva interazione venga inserita nella tua coda. Un conto alla rovescia di 5 secondi viene visualizzato sullo schermo non appena la pausa di 1 minuto si conclude. Una volta completata, la finestra della pausa si chiude automaticamente e il tuo stato cambia in Disponibile, a indicare che sei pronto per la successiva interazione.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle7](https://help.webex.com/images/video-seven-home.png)
Generazione di riepiloghi degli spazi
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Se sei occupato o ti devi allontanare dall'ufficio, restare aggiornato su tutti i tuoi spazi può essere difficoltoso. L'Assistente AI può generare riepiloghi degli spazi per consentirti di rimanere aggiornato su messaggi e conversazioni persi in uno spazio. Resta informato su decisioni, punti chiave e ottieni un rapido aggiornamento sulla discussione.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle8](https://help.webex.com/images/video-four-home.png)
Miglioramento dell'efficienza con trascrizioni in tempo reale
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Esperienza cliente
Le trascrizioni in tempo reale sono concepite per aiutarti a comunicare in modo più efficiente durante le interazioni con i clienti. Fornire trascrizioni in diretta al tuo Agent Desktop può aiutarti a migliorare focus, chiarezza e accuratezza delle risposte. L'obiettivo è migliorare la tua comprensione e l'efficienza durante le chiamate, riducendo le incomprensioni e aiutandoti a offrire interazioni di alta qualità che migliorano le prestazioni e la soddisfazione dei clienti.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle9](https://help.webex.com/images/video-nine-home.png)
Uso della lavagna nelle riunioni
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/product.svg)
Webex Suite
Se sei occupato o ti devi allontanare dall'ufficio, restare aggiornato su tutti i tuoi spazi può essere difficoltoso. L'Assistente AI può generare riepiloghi degli spazi per consentirti di rimanere aggiornato su messaggi e conversazioni persi in uno spazio. Resta informato su decisioni, punti chiave e ottieni un rapido aggiornamento sulla discussione.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle10](https://help.webex.com/images/video-ten-home.png)
Risposta con sicurezza tramite l'assistenza in tempo reale
![Icona prodotto](https://help.webex.com/images/helpdesk.svg)
Esperienza cliente
L'assistenza in tempo reale è una funzione di Cisco AI Assistant progettata per supportarti durante le interazioni in diretta con i clienti che ti consente di comprendere costantemente il contesto della conversazione, identificare l'intento del cliente e ottenere immediatamente consigli pertinenti. Ti guida in tempo reale consigliandoti cosa dire o cosa fare, aiutandoti a eseguire procedure complesse e persino avviando automaticamente flussi di lavoro.
![play](https://help.webex.com/images/play-icon.svg)![footer.videoTitle1](https://help.webex.com/images/video-one-home.png)

Comunità Webex

![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)

Webex Insider

![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)

![](https://help.webex.com/images/whiteArrow.svg)
